## 文档与范围 / Document and coverage

主文档是[生成、共同实现与伦理对照表](../../../../theories/becoming/results/GENERATIVITY-CHAIN.md)。首版包含62项导航：8项根基与正面论断、8项延续与共同实现、6项伦理根据与情境理由、20项学习—关切—良知—智慧及应用、14项形式支撑、6项原话与证据入口。它们是不同类型的维护单元，不是62项独立的已证理论，也不是全部历史来源的逐句翻译。

The first edition has 62 entries: 8 grounding/positive claims, 8 continuation/co-realization entries, 6 ethical/situated claims, 20 developmental and application links, 14 formal-support packages and 6 provenance/evidence entries. These are different maintenance units, not 62 independently proved theories. The scope is the connected chain confirmed by Ren, rather than every branch of the repository.

复用[总成果目录](../../../../theories/becoming/results/README.md)所指的同一原文，另按本问题的论证关系组织；没有改写现有目录生成器、阅读JSON或其生成分卷。主表链接当前理论、研究整合、评审、源码和原有检查记录。该引用句及其英文是AI候选表述，不重新署作Ren原话。

The table points to the same original research as the broader results catalogue, organizing it around the requested argumentative chain. Existing catalogue data and generated chapters remain untouched. The quoted sentence and its English translation remain an AI-authored candidate formulation.

## 编辑来源 / Editable sources

- [配对数据 / Paired data](../../../../theories/becoming/results/data/generativity-chain.json)：双语行、稳定ID、相对链接、内容更新日期、已读来源哈希、已检查页面清单。
- [生成与检查脚本 / Renderer and checker](build_generative_chain.py)：生成同一 Markdown 文档，检查配对字段、ID、文件、章节锚点、来源版本及输出一致性。
- [用户指令 / User source](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-08-bilingual-generativity-chain-index.md)：保留原始范围与历史授权；每日安排已由后续指令取消。

数据中的 `reviewed_sources` 只是已核读版本的指纹，不能证明摘要正确。`known_discovery_paths` 只表明哪些文件路径已经检查是否进入本表，不表示所有这些文档都已全文重读、属于本表范围或已经证明。

Source fingerprints record reviewed versions; they do not establish semantic correctness. The discovery inventory records inspected page paths, not full-text reading or inclusion of every page. Watch existing linked files plus `RESEARCH.md`, and inspect newly added current-theory/root-foundation pages for relevance.

## 研究后的维护流程 / Post-research maintenance workflow

1. 从仓库指令与当前 `RESEARCH.md` 开始，检查 Git 状态，保留其他会话的工作。运行 `--changes` 查看已关联来源的变化及新增/删除页面。
2. 重读变化的原文；新页面先判断是否进入本理论链。核对当前结论、明示假设、反例、中文与英文意义、原有回执与当前源码是否仍对应。只有来源真的支持，才升级完成状态。
3. 如有相关变化，编辑同一 JSON 中的两种语言、状态和链接；新增相关命题可以增加行，旧结果被替代时保留可追溯入口，不抹去反例。只有内容或链接改变，才更新内容日期及本记录的实质变更条目。
4. 对实际重读且已修订或确认的文件使用 `--record-review`；检查新增/删除文件的相关性后使用 `--record-discovery-review`。不要只刷新哈希掩盖未读的变化。
5. 运行 `--render` 与 `--check`，确认本地链接、锚点及生成内容。必要时在仓库首页或主理论入口保留导航，但不重新生成或重写并行维护的总成果目录。
6. 没有实质变化则不改表、不重复运行Lean或经验实验。维护任务不自动延伸理论、不向其他聊天发送消息、不更改原始来源，也不提交其他会话的文件。

Read changed sources before updating both languages. Preserve conditional scope, counterexamples and provenance. A changed fingerprint is a prompt to review, not permission to assert a stronger conclusion. With no relevant content change, leave the table unchanged and avoid redundant proof or experiment runs. Index maintenance does not authorize new research, messaging other chats or rewriting parallel catalogues.

从仓库根目录使用 / From the repository root:

```sh
python3 _process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/build_generative_chain.py --changes
python3 _process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/build_generative_chain.py --record-review path/to/actually-reviewed-source.md
python3 _process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/build_generative_chain.py --record-discovery-review
python3 _process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/build_generative_chain.py --render
python3 _process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/build_generative_chain.py --check
```

`--record-review` 的示例路径需要替换为实际已读文件；它不自动更正文字。渲染会拒绝未确认的已关联来源变化。新增页面仍由维护者判断，不自动复制章节或新增结论。

## 日程已取消 / Schedule cancelled

Ren 随后要求：“把之前每天更新的规则去掉”。已通过应用工具删除“更新中英生成理论链”自动任务（原标识 `automation`），每日09:00检查不再执行。仓库指令、阅读文档及生成器已移除有效的每日安排；现有文档和研究发生变化时的同步维护保留。

Ren subsequently requested removal of the daily update rule. The app tool deleted “更新中英生成理论链” (former identifier `automation`), ending the daily 09:00 check. Repository instructions, the reading document and its renderer no longer prescribe daily upkeep. The document and maintenance during relevant research remain available.

[后续原话 / Follow-up directive](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-08-cancel-daily-theory-chain-update.md)取代先前的日程授权；原始请求与初次检查回执保留历史范围，不代表仍有活动日程。 / The follow-up supersedes the scheduling authorization; the original request and initial receipt remain historical records, not active schedules.

## G0 与知识更新 / G0 and knowledge update

**并行变动核读 / Concurrent source review.** 知识更新期间另核读生成根基、统一伦理、生命依存及其整合相对版本库的全部新段落；保留其他维护者的条目与证明数量。C19、F14 两种语言仍写实践判断在评审中，现按已读当前完成记录改为明确公理范围已通过评审，不改变其旧比较定理范围。 / Read the changed passages of the shared grounding, ethical and living-dependency sources while preserving concurrent rows and proof scopes. Corrected stale pending-review wording in both languages of C19/F14 to the already recorded explicit-axiom acceptance; no new independent review is claimed here.

**知识后续，2026-10-08 / Knowledge continuation.** M21 更新为命题知识、解释性理解与技能分别说明的当前专章，链接实际编译的20项精确对应定理／34项声明审计及四项有范围的原典比较。认识吸收、能力及相关错误范围仍未完成；G0 保持伦理方向，不冒称其单独定义知识。核读本轮新增论证、源码、回执和自审后更新对应版本；不代替其他会话核准其未读变动。 / Revised M21 with the fuller knowledge account, 20 checked theorems/34 audits and scoped primary comparisons. M21 成对接入[既有研究§17](SCIENTIFIC-INQUIRY-CONDITIONS.md#17-stronger-foundations-and-the-assessment-of-scientific-standing)、证据§9和科学哲学§15。三项一手材料限定核读与九类概念反审把真伪、证据质量、科学性和纠错分开；不以接受本理论定义科学，不把容纳全部结果当作经验支持。/ This is interpretation and author counterreview, with no new theorem, experiment, independent result or cross-domain classifier validation. The same full-core target stays open. [Review](SCIENTIFIC-INQUIRY-DEMARCATION-REVIEW.json) records preservation of all three prior document prefixes and three distinct inherited source/receipt bindings.

The standard renderer supplies only M21; all 77 links and seven read/authored source versions were checked, preserving 93 other entries, other rendered bytes, discovery and unreviewed fingerprints. Focused rendering and checking of the three affected records passed with zero errors or selected source drift. Whole-chain review remains pending 42 other changed source versions and four previously unversioned links; no unrelated source was refreshed. Whole-registry checks remain separate from the completed scoped work.

## 全链复核：一般规律之后 / Whole-chain review after the general laws — 14 October 2026

按 Ren 2026-10-14 对未决问题计划的批准（“please, do all”，D 项），逐项读过本链指纹的 96 项变动来源，以及 5 个新链接或此前未入指纹的页面，并与对应条目核对。中英成对修订的条目：F30、M25（恢复设备与停止连接是对每条部分律成立的一般规律的实例）；M20（着陆一侧是一般到达律的实例，有限支持阻断是耗竭层律的实例，支持覆盖是持续律的梯级族，修复锚点是损失匹配计数律的实例）；C14（复制机是一般复制律的实例，规则继承与实践训练是门控改变之律的实例）；M24（修订经存量的一般规律运行）；M26（绑定结果是对每类记录与历史成立的律，学习方式的传递对每次接触成立；“同一账”“新成本账”改用结构性用语）；M15（“同等信用”改为“同等地位”）；C01 新增指向当前脊柱章的链接。其余变动经核为改名、哈希、生成页，或不改变条目所说的扩展，相应条目确认不变。发现清单：38 个新页面中只纳入 THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING.md；评审、计划与清单不作条目根据；G0-WARRANT.md 未纳入，因为它仍称 G0 为采用的公理，它与后来折叠的关系由 Ren 决定；两个“移除”页面仍在，只是不在发现范围内。旧机器条目中的市场用语（付费执行、计费读取、成本上限等）按 Ren 的选择随旧机器名保留，列给 Ren。这是作者复核，不是独立验收，不证明新命题。

On Ren's approval of the open-issues plan on 14 October 2026 ("please, do all", item D), each of the 96 changed sources this chain fingerprints, and five pages newly linked or not yet fingerprinted, was read against its paired entries. Revised entries, both languages: F30 and M25 (the recovery device and the stopping connection are instances of general laws for every partial law); M20 (the landing side is an instance of the general reaching law, the finite-support obstruction of the law of drained levels, the support cover a ladder of the lasting law, and the repair anchors instances of the counting law of loss matching); C14 (the copy machines are instances of the general copying laws, rule inheritance and practice training of the gated-change law); M24 (revision runs through the general laws of stock); M26 (the binding results are laws for every type of records and histories, and the transmission law holds of every exposure; "one account" and "cost account" are replaced by structural words); M15 ("equal credit" becomes "stand equally"); C01 gains a link to the current spine chapter. The other changes are renames, hashes, generated pages, or extensions that leave their entries right; those entries are confirmed. Discovery: of 38 new pages only THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING.md is linked; reviews, plans and inventories are not grounds for entries; G0-WARRANT.md is not linked, because it still calls G0 an adopted axiom and its relation to the later fold is Ren's to settle; the two "removed" pages still exist outside the discovery globs. Market words in entries about old machines (paid execution, charged reads, cost ceiling and the like) stay with the old machines' names, by Ren's choice, and are listed for Ren. This is author review, not independent acceptance, and proves no new claim.

## 全链复核之后所刷新指纹的复读 / Reading the fingerprints refreshed after the whole-chain review — 16 October 2026

按第十八次审查（R18）的发现 P8 与修正清单第 37 项（[审查](../history/root-foundation/BECOMING-EIGHTEENTH-REVIEW.md)）。全链复核（`e667f358`）之后，到 `25560d2d` 为止有 34 次提交改动本链数据（33 次落地，与 P129 对 C03 的改写），刷新了 `reviewed_sources` 中 78 项来源的指纹，本记录此前没有相应条目。逐次以 git log 与 `generative_chain.py --check` 核对。

**复读的证据。** 以“提交改动了成对条目，或其研究一节写明链复核”为准：只有 `25560d2d`（P129）改动了成对条目 C03（G0 是以 §6.15 的条件为前提的定理，理论不断言这一条件）；研究第 134–161 节都没有写链复核。26 次提交的说明写“chain reviewed, no entry affected”或“chain review recorded”，其中四次写明复读的来源数（`d8236061` 35 项、`13161a90` 43 项、`1a82808d` 34 项、`04238747` 40 项）；8 次落地刷新了指纹而没有任何复读的记载：`6dbf6b79`（P110–P112）、`2f95dda3`（I4b-4）、`8188bc30`（P119）、`841527bd`（I4b-2）、`3b7f1c98`（P118）、`94fdd24e`（I4b-2 的后续）、`09218b82`（P120）、`ff4d5f17`（P121、P122）。

**本次复读。** 78 项来源自 `e667f358` 至今的净改动都已读过，并与成对条目核对：
- 生成页 22 项（RESEARCH.md、theories/becoming/README.md、CLAIMS.md 与 19 个记录页）：新记录的行、改绑的回执行与沿革注。成对条目确认不变。
- 记录 JSON 33 项：29 项只改 aliases、history、proof_bindings、references 或 related。改动内容的四项：historical-source-binding 的 claim（跨一段日期的控制只在关系取两个值、两端读数相同时定下其间各日期）；inherited-structural-copying 的 claim（以不同相邻对排序限于同一长度的词）；learned-procedure-transmission 的三条新律与依存（容量是一个共享池所释放的最长世系，物质与支持沿每段经历守恒，Price 方程的计数形式）；growth-constrained-fragmentation 新增对 risk-certificate-outcome-distinction 的 formal-reuse。成对条目 M26、C14 不说被收窄的细节，M26 所说的按容量造出后继、守恒物质与支持与新律一致。确认不变。
- Lean 源 21 项：12 个外围模块只改第 1 行文件头注（I4c，指向脊柱上的声明）；Counterfactual、Experiment、Histories、Mechanism、Relevance、Response 只改注释（读法由“待 Ren”改为依交托采用，P110）；RepairComparison 把 `fragile_b3_below_nothing` 改名为 `fragile_b3_better_than_none`，陈述不变；Unfolding 增 `same_steps_iff_traces` 及其文档；Bridge 增 I3a–I3e 的桥接定理（新增 986 行，删去的只是两行文档）。链的数据中不出现旧名。F03、F04、F12、F20、F22、C01、M15、M20 确认不变。
- CROSSED-CLAIMS-ARGUMENT.md：同一改名，“strictly matched below”改为“strictly better by matching than”，意义不变。M20 确认不变。
- THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING.md：新模块在 §1–§3、§8、§9 与术语表中的段与行，P110 的读法状态，P128 的折叠（第二部分成为 §6.15 的条件小节）与 P129。C01 的断言是一般的，确认不变；C03 已由 `25560d2d` 改写。

**发现，未在此改动。** M15 的范围仍写“G0当前由第二部分Unrestricted推出”/“G0 now follows from Part II’s Unrestricted”。P128 之后应读作：G0 以 §6.15 的条件（Unrestricted）为前提，理论不断言这一条件，同 C03 的改写。M15 的成对来源不链接章节，所以指纹没有显示这一处；按本链的流程由整合者改写。

**仍待复读，未刷新任何指纹。** `--check` 现报：章节、ROADMAP.md 与 recurring-reference-revision.md 在 `25560d2d` 之后又改（P129 中最小的识一段，与第十八次审查的合并），以及五个新页（第十八次审查、发布准备度复核、DELEGATED-CHOICES.md、MODULE-CLASSIFICATION.md、THEOREM-CONNECTIVITY.md）。分支 `r18-record-fixes` 改动两个记录标题（observation-is-a-route、loops-within-what-a-law-reads），生成的登记页随之改动。这些留给下一次链复核。`Parting`、`Observation`、`Selection` 是否进入链中学习与智慧的环节，由 Ren 决定（修正清单第 51 项）。

这是作者复读，不是独立验收，不证明新命题。

Following finding P8 and fix-list item 37 of the eighteenth review (R18, [review](../history/root-foundation/BECOMING-EIGHTEENTH-REVIEW.md)). After the whole-chain review (`e667f358`), 34 commits up to `25560d2d` changed this chain's data (33 landings and P129's revision of C03) and refreshed 78 fingerprints in `reviewed_sources`, with no entry here. Each was checked with git log and `generative_chain.py --check`.

**Evidence of reading.** Taking as evidence a commit that changed the paired entry, or a study section stating the chain review: only `25560d2d` (P129) changed a paired entry, C03 (G0 is a theorem with the condition of section 6.15, which the theory does not assert); study sections 134–161 state no chain review. Twenty-six commit messages say “chain reviewed, no entry affected” or “chain review recorded”, four of them with the number of sources reread (`d8236061` 35, `13161a90` 43, `1a82808d` 34, `04238747` 40). Eight landings refreshed fingerprints with no record of reading: `6dbf6b79` (P110–P112), `2f95dda3` (I4b-4), `8188bc30` (P119), `841527bd` (I4b-2), `3b7f1c98` (P118), `94fdd24e` (the I4b-2 follow-up), `09218b82` (P120) and `ff4d5f17` (P121, P122).

**Read now.** The net change since `e667f358` of all 78 sources was read against its paired entries:
- 22 generated pages (RESEARCH.md, theories/becoming/README.md, CLAIMS.md and 19 record pages): rows of new records, rebound receipt rows and history notes. Paired entries confirmed.
- 33 record JSON files: 29 change only aliases, history, proof_bindings, references or related. Four change content: historical-source-binding's claim (controls across a span of dates settle the dates between only where the relation takes two values and both ends read the same); inherited-structural-copying's claim (the ranking by unlike adjacent pairs is among words of one length); learned-procedure-transmission's three new laws and dependencies (the capacity is the longest lineage one shared pool releases, material and support are conserved along every course of exposures, and the counting form of Price's equation); growth-constrained-fragmentation's new formal reuse of risk-certificate-outcome-distinction. The paired entries M26 and C14 do not state the narrowed details, and M26's successors built as capacity allows, with material and support conserved, agree with the new laws. Confirmed.
- 21 Lean sources: twelve periphery modules change only a line-1 header comment (I4c, pointing to the spine declarations); Counterfactual, Experiment, Histories, Mechanism, Relevance and Response change only comments (readings moved from pending Ren to adopted on the delegation, P110); RepairComparison renames `fragile_b3_below_nothing` to `fragile_b3_better_than_none`, the statement unchanged; Unfolding adds `same_steps_iff_traces` and its documentation; Bridge adds the I3a–I3e bridge theorems (986 lines added, two documentation lines removed). No old name occurs in the chain's data. F03, F04, F12, F20, F22, C01, M15 and M20 confirmed.
- CROSSED-CLAIMS-ARGUMENT.md: the same rename, and “strictly matched below” becomes “strictly better by matching than”, with the same meaning. M20 confirmed.
- THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING.md: the new modules' paragraphs and rows in sections 1–3, 8, 9 and the glossary, P110's reading status, P128's fold (Part II becomes the conditional section 6.15) and P129. C01's claim is general and is confirmed; C03 was revised by `25560d2d`.

**Found, not changed here.** M15's scope still says “G0 now follows from Part II’s Unrestricted” / “G0当前由第二部分Unrestricted推出”. After P128 it should read that G0 follows with the condition of section 6.15 (Unrestricted), which the theory does not assert, as C03 now reads. M15's paired sources do not link the chapter, so no fingerprint showed it; the integrator revises it through this chain's workflow.

**Still to read; no fingerprint refreshed.** `--check` now reports the chapter, ROADMAP.md and recurring-reference-revision.md changed after `25560d2d` (P129's paragraph on the least registration, and the merge of the eighteenth review), and five new pages (the eighteenth review, the release-readiness review, DELEGATED-CHOICES.md, MODULE-CLASSIFICATION.md and THEOREM-CONNECTIVITY.md). Branch `r18-record-fixes` changes two record titles (observation-is-a-route, loops-within-what-a-law-reads), and the generated registers change with them. These are left for the next chain review. Whether `Parting`, `Observation` and `Selection` enter the chain's learning and wisdom links is Ren's to decide (fix-list item 51).

This is author reading, not independent acceptance, and proves no new claim.

## 学习与智慧的环节，与 10 月 16 日以来变动的来源 / The learning and wisdom links, and the sources changed since 16 October — 18 October 2026

按 Ren 2026-10-18 的回答（“I take the revised readings of 生生, 生生不息 and "entangled". Record my answer in sources, then apply them with Part B's recommendations, coordinating with N6.”，[原话](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-18-central-readings-taken.md)；发自 AI 起草的回复按钮，措辞是 AI 的，选择是 Ren 的）：他取了[决定简报](../history/root-foundation/READINGS-PENDING-REN.md#b-the-other-pending-readings) B 部分第 19 项，记为 [D43](DELEGATED-CHOICES.md#d43-其余的待决读法--the-other-pending-readings)；工作板 A2；第十八次审查的发现 P8 与修正清单第 37、51 项（[审查](../history/root-foundation/BECOMING-EIGHTEENTH-REVIEW.md)）。

**Ren 所取的环节。** 两个条目各以中英两种语言追加，各增五个链接；其他条目的文字不变（[apply_chain_links.py](../history/root-foundation/development/a2-chain/apply_chain_links.py)，只追加）。
- 智慧（M15），`Selection`：选择经选择者的模型，不经记录。模型是备择的映射，每个经它的选择把两个备择选得相同，恰当它使二者并列（`Relabel.selected_alike_iff`）；经一个归并的选择对要紧者正确，只在要紧者跨这一归并同去之处；归并合起了要紧者所分开的两个备择时，没有一个正确（`Learning.selects_right_through`、`Learning.selects_not_right_of_parted`）。条目引 Ren 2026-10-16 的原话“it is about the cognitive model, not just record”（[来源](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-16-selection-through-a-model.md)）；简报中的“memory is not wisdom”是 AI 的转述，不作为他的话引用。
- 学习（M26），`Observation` 与 `Parting`：观察在下游之内（`apart_only_downstream`、`exchange_alike_off_downstream`），读法按 D43 所取减弱；“观察不是依赖之外的第二种关系”是猜想，不在这一环节之内。运行分开之处，前段与律都不固定其去向（`Parting.not_right_on_both`）；移动与配置列出、步已定时，依过程所建之外推恰在没有两个运行分开之处正确（`Parting.world_right_iff`）；运行不分开之处，按最小界的外推从 2N − 1 起正确（`Parting.leastBound_right`）。

新句子用[命名研究](REGISTRATION-VOCABULARY-STUDY.md#7-decisions-18-october-2026)采用的用语（耦合入、映射、前段、外推、备择），不用“registers”“registering”；链中先前的文字保留原词，N6 不改链。九个新链接目标（三项记录、`Selection.lean`、`Learning.lean`、`Observation.lean`、`Parting.lean`、Ren 10 月 16 日的来源与 DELEGATED-CHOICES.md）已读，指纹已记入。

**P8 的 78 项指纹。** 已于 2026-10-16 读过，记在上一条（`e3b08cda9`，修正清单第 37 项）。此后没有指纹被刷新：迁移批次 H4、H5、H7 把 333 项指纹的键改为文件的新路径，哈希不变（`9ae84ae52` 35 项、`71edc7b91` 297 项、`19c1acfa4` 1 项）；P129 落地（`4cc696aef`）按上一条所请改写了 M15 中关于 G0 的一句，没有改动指纹。

**此后变动的来源。** `--check` 当时报 261 项来源变动、50 个新页，来自 10 月 16–17 日的重组（迁移 H1–H7 与 RA–RG）、语言分离、10 月 17 日的改标（G0 → 生命意义；第一、二部分 → 无公理的理论与条件小节；那一条公理 → 延续条件）、N1 改名（N5）以及其后的落地。[classify_pending.py](../history/root-foundation/development/a2-chain/classify_pending.py) 把每项与其指纹所记、在 git 历史中找到的版本比较；[reading.json](../history/root-foundation/development/a2-chain/reading.json) 列出每项来源的类别、所属条目与所见。刷新 203 项：
- 146 项把链接目标抹去后文字相同。[audit_links.py](../history/root-foundation/development/a2-chain/audit_links.py) 核对每个改写的目标指向同一文件的新路径；迁移清单所记的改写（H1、H3、H4、H5）把每个指纹接到当前哈希：全部改动出自迁移工具。
- 2 项抹去链接后等于语言分离对所记版本的英文输出；其中文在中文版中。
- 23 项记录 JSON 只改 history、proof_bindings 与 references（回执改绑、引用重新指向、带日期的注）：主张与范围不变。
- 25 个以只用英文的模板重新生成的记录页，其记录只改上述字段、改为迁往 history 的路径，或把 P 编号换成记录名（consequence-revised-reference-plan、selection-under-registration-revision、recurring-reference-revision、finite-fallible-inquiry-necessity）；M20、M21、M24、M25、M26 与 F30 对照确认。
- A2 所扩展的两个条目共有 33 项变动来源：25 项属上列各类，8 项对照链接它们的每个条目通读，其中 7 项刷新：WISDOM-AND-CONTEXT.md（只有中文的段落现已译入英文版；改标）、COGNITION-LEARNING-AND-EVOLUTION.md（改标；去掉 P73、P74）、finite-fallible-inquiry-necessity.json（“P94 tests balance”改为“A test of balance”；回执改绑），以及 Lean 源 Relevance（文档注释改标）、Response、Counterfactual、ContinuingRevision（N5 改名；Counterfactual 的文档注释写出其陈述原有的双重否定，`ExchangeLaw.world` 改经 `World.ofKind` 与新的 `ExchangeLaw.courseWeb` 写出）。N5 改过的名字都不出现在链的数据中。M15、M26 确认。KNOWLEDGE.md 仍待（见下）。
落地时只在文件仍是所读版本处记入指纹（[record_reading.py](../history/root-foundation/development/a2-chain/record_reading.py)），或在其唯一的改动是 A2 自己的落地给记录页历史所加的重新指向注之处。A2 读后、落地前，F1、G3、L2 与 C1、U2c、M3、F3 sweep step 1、W1 与 F2（连同其后续）的落地改动了其中十五项来源。十三项是记录文件，其改动是改绑与重新指向：证明引用指向新文件而声明不变（F1 的 Bridge.lean，W1 的 Stock.lean），证明绑定改绑到新的核心回执，声明连同全名移动（W1 把 Flow、Web 与 total 移入 World.lean；F2 把 derived-history-law-exactness 的规范来源移入核心的 Readout.lean 与 SummaryFutures.lean），以及回执行、一条“Used by”链接与带日期的注。ContinuingRevision.lean 加了 W1 对 Becoming.Labelled 的导入，没有陈述改动。DELEGATED-CHOICES.md 加了 L2 对 D40 的扩展、C1 的 D44 与 U2c 对连接处的结果，M15、M26 所引的 D43 的读法不变。这些改动已读过，没有条目的论断或范围受影响，指纹按所读版本记入（[reread_at_landing.py](../history/root-foundation/development/a2-chain/reread_at_landing.py)）；未读过改动的来源仍待。

**仍待复读，58 项，未刷新任何指纹。** KNOWLEDGE.md 已读：M15 与之相合，但 M21 仍称其两个回答为公理性的，而该章现说生命意义在延续条件之下推出。E06 所链的 CLAIMS.md 现在只是指向 RESULTS.md 的存根。ROADMAP.md（M20）、RESEARCH.md 与 theories/becoming/README.md（登记页；后两者无条目链接）。20 个文字在语言分离之外有改动（译文与改标）的章节与研究：标准章（C01、C03）、BECOMING-FOUNDATION（C01）、THEORY-OF-BECOMING（C09）、PRACTICAL-JUDGMENT（C17–C19、C23、C24、M14、M20）、ZERO-AXIOM-PRACTICAL-JUDGMENT 及其整合（C20、C26、F18、F23、F26–F28）、RELATIONS-ACROSS-SCALES（C28、C29）、PLURAL-SYSTEMS-AND-CORRECTION（C33、F29、M23）、GENERATED-LOGIC（F01）、ALIGNMENT-THROUGH-CHANGE（F30、M25）、CROSSED-CLAIMS-ARGUMENT 及其整合、ALIGNMENT-DIRECTION、BASIC-CONFLICT-JUDGMENT、CHOICE-AND-GENERATIVE-CONTEXT、ETHICAL-LEARNING 与 MARKETS-AND-GENERATIVE-REGIMES（M20）、COLLECTIVE-DESTRUCTIVE-ATTRACTORS 与 COMMON-ORGANIZATION（M24）、SCIENTIFIC-PHILOSOPHY（无条目）。19 项内容有改动的其他来源：Lean 源（M20 的 Bridge；M21 的 FallibleInquiry 与 TimedResponse；M24 的 ReferencePlan 与 Revision；无条目链接的 Unfolding）、记录（M20 的 open-form-realization-under-revision、F01 的 constitutive-binding-assessment，与三项无条目链接者）、LIANGZHI-EVOLUTION（M12–M14）、THE-THEORY-OF-EVOLUTION（C14）、MATHEMATICAL-CORE（F01）、EMPIRICAL-SCOPE（E06）、PHILOSOPHICAL-ACCOUNT（M21），以及无条目链接的 check_fallible_inquiry.py 与两个 README。14 项没有哪个已提交的版本具有所记的哈希：C30 与 F22 的七项记录、organizational-formation-from-generative-comparison（M20），与六项无条目链接的记录。这些留给下一次整链复核。

**发现清单。** 50 个新页中，38 个是语言分离产生的中文版，经其英文版到达；DELEGATED-CHOICES.md 现已链接（D43）；其余 11 个是评审（第十八次评审、发布准备度评审与评审索引）、研究、计划、清单与索引（BECOMING-BRIDGE-FOLD、INDEX、MODULE-CLASSIFICATION、THEOREM-CONNECTIVITY、READINGS-PENDING-REN、命名研究、章节 README、参考目录），不作条目根据。它们加入已检清单；之后才发现的页面不加入。

**渲染。** 仍有待读来源时 `--render` 拒绝，所以 GENERATIVITY-CHAIN.md 在 `validate` 通过后只从数据渲染（[render_chain.py](../history/root-foundation/development/a2-chain/render_chain.py)），同 P129。

**发现，未在此改动。** (1) M21 的“the two axiomatic answers remain complete”与“does not prove the opposite axiom”：P128 与改标之后，这两个回答在延续条件之下成立，理论不断言这一条件，同 P129 对 M15、C03 的改写。(2) 链的条目保留已退役的标签（G0、J1–J4、第一／第二部分、那一条公理）与 P 编号；AGENTS.md 要求现行文字用现行用语，而 N6 不改 Ren 所编的论证，所以链的改标由 Ren 决定。(3) CLAIMS.md（E06）是存根；登记页是 RESULTS.md。(4) 简报第 3、19 项把“memory is not wisdom”归于 Ren；第 19 项的注现说明这是 AI 的话。

这是作者复读，不是独立验收，不证明新命题。

On Ren's answer of 18 October 2026 (“I take the revised readings of 生生, 生生不息 and "entangled". Record my answer in sources, then apply them with Part B's recommendations, coordinating with N6.”, [words](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-18-central-readings-taken.md); sent from a reply button the AI drafted, so the wording is the AI's and the choice Ren's), which took item 19 of Part B of the [decision brief](../history/root-foundation/READINGS-PENDING-REN.md#b-the-other-pending-readings), recorded as [D43](DELEGATED-CHOICES.md#d43-其余的待决读法--the-other-pending-readings); board item A2; finding P8 and fix-list items 37 and 51 of the eighteenth review ([review](../history/root-foundation/BECOMING-EIGHTEENTH-REVIEW.md)).

**The links Ren took.** Appended to two entries in both languages, with five links each; no other entry's text changed ([apply_chain_links.py](../history/root-foundation/development/a2-chain/apply_chain_links.py), which only appends).
- Wisdom (M15), `Selection`: selection works through the selector's model, not the record. With the model a map of the alternatives, every selection through it selects two alternatives alike exactly when it ties them (`Relabel.selected_alike_iff`); a selection through a lumping is right about what matters only where what matters goes alike across the lumping, and where the lumping joins two alternatives that what matters separates, none is (`Learning.selects_right_through`, `Learning.selects_not_right_of_parted`). The entry quotes Ren's words of 16 October, “it is about the cognitive model, not just record” ([source](../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-16-selection-through-a-model.md)); "memory is not wisdom", the brief's phrase, is the AI's paraphrase and is not quoted as his.
- Learning (M26), `Observation` and `Parting`: observing lies within the downstream (`apart_only_downstream`, `exchange_alike_off_downstream`), the reading weakened as D43 took it; that observation is no second relation beside dependence is a conjecture and stays out of the link. Where runs part, neither the prefix nor the law fixes the way (`Parting.not_right_on_both`); with listed moves and configurations and decided steps, the extrapolation built from the process is right exactly where no two runs part (`Parting.world_right_iff`); where runs do not part, the extrapolation by the least bound is right from 2N − 1 on (`Parting.leastBound_right`).

The new sentences use the vocabulary the [naming study](REGISTRATION-VOCABULARY-STUDY.md#7-decisions-18-october-2026) adopted (couples into, the map, prefix, extrapolation, alternatives), not "registers" or "registering"; the chain's earlier text keeps its words, which N6 does not edit. The nine new link targets (the three records, `Selection.lean`, `Learning.lean`, `Observation.lean`, `Parting.lean`, Ren's source of 16 October and DELEGATED-CHOICES.md) were read and their fingerprints recorded.

**P8's 78 fingerprints.** They were read on 16 October 2026 and recorded in the entry above (`e3b08cda9`, fix-list item 37). No fingerprint was refreshed after it: the moves H4, H5 and H7 re-keyed 333 fingerprints to their files' new paths with the same hashes (`9ae84ae52` 35, `71edc7b91` 297, `19c1acfa4` 1), and P129's landing (`4cc696aef`) rewrote M15's sentence on G0 as that entry asked, with no fingerprint changed.

**The sources changed since.** `--check` then reported 261 changed sources and 50 new pages, from the restructure of 16–17 October (moves H1–H7 and RA–RG), the language separation, the relabel of 17 October (G0 → living significance; Part I/II → the theory without axiom and the conditional section; the one axiom → the continuation condition), the N1 renaming (N5) and the landings since. [classify_pending.py](../history/root-foundation/development/a2-chain/classify_pending.py) compared each with the version its fingerprint records, found in git history; [reading.json](../history/root-foundation/development/a2-chain/reading.json) lists every source with its kind, its entries and what was found. Refreshed, 203:
- 146 whose text is unchanged once link targets are blanked. [audit_links.py](../history/root-foundation/development/a2-chain/audit_links.py) checks that every rewritten target names the same file at its new path, and the rewrites the move manifests record (H1, H3, H4, H5) take each fingerprint to the current hash: the whole change is the move tool's.
- 2 equal, links blanked, to the language separation's English output of the recorded version; their Chinese is in the Chinese editions.
- 23 record JSON files whose changed fields are only history, proof_bindings and references (receipts rebound, references re-pointed, dated notes): claim and scope unchanged.
- 25 record pages regenerated in the English-only template, whose records changed only in those fields, in paths moved to history, or in wording that replaces a P-number with the record's name (consequence-revised-reference-plan, selection-under-registration-revision, recurring-reference-revision, finite-fallible-inquiry-necessity); M20, M21, M24, M25, M26 and F30 confirmed against them.
- Of the 33 changed sources of the two entries A2 extended, 25 are of the kinds above and 8 were read in full against every entry linking them; 7 are refreshed: WISDOM-AND-CONTEXT.md (the Chinese-only passages now translated in the English edition; the relabel), COGNITION-LEARNING-AND-EVOLUTION.md (the relabel; P73 and P74 removed), finite-fallible-inquiry-necessity.json (“P94 tests balance” becomes “A test of balance”; receipts rebound), and the Lean sources Relevance (docstrings relabelled), Response, Counterfactual and ContinuingRevision (N5 renames; Counterfactual's docstring now states the double negation its statement had, and `ExchangeLaw.world` is written through `World.ofKind` with the new `ExchangeLaw.courseWeb`). No name N5 renamed occurs in the chain's data. M15 and M26 confirmed. KNOWLEDGE.md stays pending (below).
A fingerprint is recorded at landing only where the file is still the version read ([record_reading.py](../history/root-foundation/development/a2-chain/record_reading.py)), or where its only change is the re-pointing note A2's own landing adds to a record page's history. Between A2's reading and its landing, the landings of F1, G3, L2 and C1, U2c, M3, F3 sweep step 1, W1 and F2 (with its follow-up) changed fifteen of these sources. Thirteen are record files whose changes are rebinding and re-pointing: proof references re-pointed with the same declarations (Bridge.lean by F1, Stock.lean by W1), proof bindings rebound to the new core receipts, declarations moved with their full names (W1 moved Flow, Web and total to World.lean; F2 moved the canonical source of derived-history-law-exactness into the core's Readout.lean and SummaryFutures.lean), receipt rows, a “Used by” link and dated notes. ContinuingRevision.lean gained W1's import of Becoming.Labelled, with no statement changed. DELEGATED-CHOICES.md gained L2's extension of D40, C1's D44 and U2c's result for the joins, while D43's readings, which M15 and M26 cite, are unchanged. Those changes were read, no entry's claim or scope is affected, and the fingerprints are recorded at the versions read ([reread_at_landing.py](../history/root-foundation/development/a2-chain/reread_at_landing.py)); a source whose change was not read stays pending.

**Still pending, 58; no fingerprint refreshed.** KNOWLEDGE.md, read: M15 agrees with it, but M21 still calls its two answers axiomatic, while the chapter now says living significance follows under the continuation condition. CLAIMS.md, which E06 links, is now a stub pointing to RESULTS.md. ROADMAP.md (M20), RESEARCH.md and theories/becoming/README.md (registers; no entry links the last two). Twenty chapters and studies whose text changed beyond the language separation (translations and the relabel): the canonical chapter (C01, C03), BECOMING-FOUNDATION (C01), THEORY-OF-BECOMING (C09), PRACTICAL-JUDGMENT (C17–C19, C23, C24, M14, M20), ZERO-AXIOM-PRACTICAL-JUDGMENT and its integration (C20, C26, F18, F23, F26–F28), RELATIONS-ACROSS-SCALES (C28, C29), PLURAL-SYSTEMS-AND-CORRECTION (C33, F29, M23), GENERATED-LOGIC (F01), ALIGNMENT-THROUGH-CHANGE (F30, M25), CROSSED-CLAIMS-ARGUMENT and its integration, ALIGNMENT-DIRECTION, BASIC-CONFLICT-JUDGMENT, CHOICE-AND-GENERATIVE-CONTEXT, ETHICAL-LEARNING and MARKETS-AND-GENERATIVE-REGIMES (M20), COLLECTIVE-DESTRUCTIVE-ATTRACTORS and COMMON-ORGANIZATION (M24), SCIENTIFIC-PHILOSOPHY (no entry). Nineteen other sources changed in content: Lean sources (Bridge for M20; FallibleInquiry and TimedResponse for M21; ReferencePlan and Revision for M24; Unfolding, which no entry links), records (open-form-realization-under-revision for M20, constitutive-binding-assessment for F01, and three no entry links), LIANGZHI-EVOLUTION (M12–M14), THE-THEORY-OF-EVOLUTION (C14), MATHEMATICAL-CORE (F01), EMPIRICAL-SCOPE (E06), PHILOSOPHICAL-ACCOUNT (M21), and check_fallible_inquiry.py and two READMEs, which no entry links. Fourteen for which no committed version has the recorded hash: seven records of C30 and F22, organizational-formation-from-generative-comparison (M20), and six records no entry links. These are left for the next whole-chain review.

**Discovery.** Of the 50 new pages, 38 are Chinese editions from the language separation, reached through their English editions; DELEGATED-CHOICES.md is now linked (D43); the other 11 are reviews (the eighteenth and release-readiness reviews and the reviews index), studies, plans, inventories and indexes (BECOMING-BRIDGE-FOLD, INDEX, MODULE-CLASSIFICATION, THEOREM-CONNECTIVITY, READINGS-PENDING-REN, the naming study, the chapters' README, the reference catalogue), which are not grounds for entries. They join the inspected inventory; a page discovered later does not.

**Rendering.** `--render` refuses while sources are pending, so GENERATIVITY-CHAIN.md is rendered from the data alone after `validate` passes ([render_chain.py](../history/root-foundation/development/a2-chain/render_chain.py)), as P129 did.

**Found, not changed here.** (1) M21 says “the two axiomatic answers remain complete” and that a criticism “does not prove the opposite axiom”; after P128 and the relabel these answers hold under the continuation condition, which the theory does not assert, as P129 revised M15 and C03. (2) The chain's entries keep the retired labels (G0, J1–J4, Part I/II, the axiom) and P-numbers; AGENTS.md puts live text in the current terms, while N6 leaves Ren's curated argument unedited, so relabelling the chain is Ren's to decide. (3) CLAIMS.md (E06) is a stub; the register is RESULTS.md. (4) The brief attributes "memory is not wisdom" to Ren in items 3 and 19; the note at item 19 now says the words are the AI's.

This is author reading, not independent acceptance, and proves no new claim.

## 复读待读的来源，四个条目的现状改写，与改标提议 / The pending sources read, four entries restated, and a relabelling proposal — 19 October 2026

工作板 A3。上一条（A2）留下 58 项待读来源，并列出发现而未改动的几处；第十八次审查的发现 P8 要求每项刷新的指纹都经阅读（[审查](../history/root-foundation/BECOMING-EIGHTEENTH-REVIEW.md)）。

**所读的来源，65 项。** A2 留下的 58 项，加上 A2 读后又变动的 7 项（F3J 与 F3Q 的移植改动了 classification-robust-generative-comparison 的记录与页面、organizational-rule-inheritance.json、transition-grouping-word-partition 的记录与页面；G4 的改绑注改动了 recurring-reference-revision.md 与 selection-through-a-model-of-the-options.md）。每项按其指纹所记、在 git 历史中找到的版本（[classify_pending.py](../history/root-foundation/development/a2-chain/classify_pending.py)），对照链接它的每个条目通读；[reading.json](../history/root-foundation/development/a3-chain/reading.json) 列出每项的类别、条目、所读版本与所见。类别：生成页 8 项；经语言分离拆分、并另有改动（译文、改标、轮次记录移出）的章节与研究 21 项；其他内容变动 22 项（记录、Lean 源、README、存根）；没有哪个已提交版本具有所记哈希者 14 项，对其条目通读现行文件。18 项没有条目链接，读其改动后刷新。65 项全部刷新，无一仍待。

**改写的条目，中英两种语言。** 只在来源已不支持条目所说之处改写；标签（G0、J1–J4、公理路线、P 编号）照 P129 保留，列入下面的提议。
- C17 主张、C24 范围：PRACTICAL-JUDGMENT 的前提表现作“Living significance | Follows under the continuation condition (canonical chapter §6.15); not assumed”，完成一段现作“under the explicit practical commitments and living significance, which follows under the continuation condition”。条目以现在时说 G0 作为公理采用、候选在“明确公理下”完整，照 P129 改写 C03 的方式改为 G0 现为以§6.15之条件为条件的定理。
- M21（A2 发现 1）：PHILOSOPHICAL-ACCOUNT §32 现作“The answers under the continuation condition and the practical account are complete at their declared scope”。“两个公理回答保持完成”改为“两个回答在§6.15的延续条件之下保持完成”；“不等于证明相反公理”改为“不等于证明其反面 G0”，并注 G0 的现状；“不反过来证明公理”改为“不反过来证明 G0 或其所出的条件”。
- M20 范围：ETHICAL-LEARNING §12 现把所形成的地位放在“the scope of life declared for living significance”之内，原作“在所采用的 G0 生命范围中”；“公理化哲学连接”改为在 G0 与 J1–J4 之下的哲学连接，并注 G0 的现状。
- 链接：E06 的 CLAIMS.md 改指 RESULTS.md（A2 发现 3），M20 的 ROADMAP.md 改指 GOALS.md#generative-alignment；两页现都是指向这两处的存根。RESULTS.md 与 GOALS.md 已读，指纹记入。
各处改动由 [apply_entries.py](../history/root-foundation/development/a3-chain/apply_entries.py) 按 [revisions.json](../history/root-foundation/development/a3-chain/revisions.json) 施行，每处替换一个只出现一次的片段，并写明来源与理由。

**确认的条目。** C01、C03、C09、C14、C18–C20、C23、C26、C28–C30、C33、F01、F18、F22、F23、F26–F30、M12–M15、M23–M26 与其来源相合。新文字多是改标（G0 → 生命意义，J1–J4 → 各项实践承诺，G0/J → 条件对齐路线）、只有中文的段落译入英文版、轮次记录逐字移入 history/，以及改绑、改名（N5）、移植（F1、F3J、F3Q、F3R）与重新指向。这些没有改动任何条目的论断或范围。C30 与 F22 的七项记录、organizational-formation-from-generative-comparison（M20）按现行文件读过：它们此后的历史只记改绑，每条都说主张不变。

**发现清单。** 新页 PHRASE-READINGS-PENDING-REN.md 是决定简报 C 部分为 Ren 准备的一次过目单（审查第 50 项），Ren 于 2026-10-19 以 D45 整体作答；本条的提议页 CHAIN-RELABELLING-PENDING-REN.md 也是待 Ren 决定之页；二者都不作条目根据，加入已检清单。

No entry links 18 of them; those were refreshed after their change was read. All 65 are refreshed; none stays pending.

**Entries revised, both languages.** An entry was revised only where its source no longer supports what it says. Labels (G0, J1–J4, the axiom route, P-numbers) are kept, as P129 kept them, and are listed in the proposal below.
- C17's claim and C24's scope. PRACTICAL-JUDGMENT's premise table now reads "Living significance | Follows under the continuation condition (canonical chapter §6.15); not assumed", and its completion paragraph "under the explicit practical commitments and living significance, which follows under the continuation condition". They now say that G0 is a theorem with the condition of section 6.15, which the theory does not assert, as P129 restated C03.
- M21 (A2's finding 1). PHILOSOPHICAL-ACCOUNT §32 now reads "The answers under the continuation condition and the practical account are complete at their declared scope". "The two axiomatic answers remain complete" becomes "the two answers remain complete under the continuation condition of section 6.15". "Does not prove the opposite axiom" becomes "does not prove its opposite, G0", with G0's status stated. "Does not prove the axiom" becomes "does not prove G0 or the condition it follows from".
- M20's scope. ETHICAL-LEARNING §12 now places formed standing "within the scope of life declared for living significance", where it read "在所采用的 G0 生命范围中". The connection "under the explicit axioms" (公理化) becomes one under G0 and J1–J4, with G0's status stated.

This entry's proposal page, CHAIN-RELABELLING-PENDING-REN.md, is also a page awaiting Ren's decision. Neither grounds an entry. Both join the inspected inventory.

## 10 月 20 日以来的整链复读：确认、提议与新页 / The chain read again since 20 October: confirmed, proposed and the new pages — 28 October 2026

工作板 GC。上次整链复读是 A3（10 月 19 日）；R35（10 月 20 日）只把移入历史的路径改键。此后 RA 的四层重构、R19–R40、DF1/DF1b/DF2 的记录更正，以及 UE、MA 等落地，使本链指纹所记的 135 项来源改动，另有 19 个新页。每项来源都从其指纹所记、在 git 历史中找到的版本读起（135 项全部找到），对照链接它的每个条目；[reading.json](development/gc-chain-review/reading.json) 逐项列出所读版本、类别、条目与所见。

**结果。** 113 项来源与其条目相合，指纹按所读版本记入。12 项暂不记入：其改动表明某个条目的文字须改，按维护规则不改条目，而列为下面的提议 A1–A3。10 项属 DF3 正在迁移的八项记录，留给 DF3 在其落地时一次复读。19 个新页读过，记入已检清单。提议 B1–B6 是条目仍然成立、但可补入新进展或现行用语之处，指纹已记。

**最要紧的提议是 A1。** 六个条目（C03、C17、C24、M15、M20、M21）说 G0（整体中的生及其继续生成的正面意义）“现为以 §6.15 之条件为条件的定理”。自 R19（10 月 19 日）起，理论按 Lean 所示陈述生生之意义：每个活的主张从每个立场看都有意义；它给出意义而非正面价值，`living` 的读法也不含继续生成；正面价值与继续生成属于生生之方向的读法（D1），由扩大可形成者比较。延续条件确定范围、不排序；排序只在 C（以广为愈）。§6.15 这个节号也已不存在（退役的规范章中为 §6.4，现行处为 07 §3）。所以这些状态句须改为同时说出两个前提。这与 10 月 19 日尚待 Ren 决定的改标提议相关：若只把 G0 改称“生生之意义”，就会把现行理论已否认的正面价值与继续生成加给它；A1 应与该提议同时或在其前施行。

**本链没有一个链接指向现行的八章。** 94 个条目中只有 12 个所链记录在八章中有其所在节；29 个条目的 39 个“current”链接指向已退役的章。B3 提议给每个条目加上现行所在节的链接，并把历史链接标为“所复读之版本”。

**11 月 3 日更正。** 英文的“所读，按类别”一段原说十四项按语言分开的来源，其后的改动都全文读过；这夸大了 10 月 28 日所读。当日全文读过的只是其中十二章的改动（各 1 至 23 行）。只在条目所用之处读过的是：已退役的规范章 THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING（R36 重构，增 1,438 行、删 1,273 行），读了 C03 所链之锚（今 §6.4 及 §6.5）与 C01 链接的标签；决定登记册，读了 M15、M26 所链的 D43 与 D45 对 where-a-level-couples 的采纳；17 个 Lean 文件，只核对本链所引之名都有声明，并按改动的类别读；GOALS 读了 M20 所链之节；RESULTS 作为由记录生成之页读。除因 A1 暂不记入的规范章外，这些都在此读法上记入了指纹。11 月 3 日读了其余部分，每项都从其指纹所记版本读到 `6c84c2d90`（[reread-2026-11-03.json](development/gc-chain-review/reread-2026-11-03.json)）：登记册的全部改动、Lean 文件的全部改动、GOALS 与 RESULTS 的全部改动，以及规范章中关乎 C01 的序言、§1.1、§2.5、§9 开头与 §11。唯一关乎条目的改动，是 D45 下为 D43 的观察猜想补入的 OB1 解答，即提议 A3（reading.json 原误记为 A2，今已改正），已由下文 GC2 施行。条目所名之定理，陈述都未改；被删的声明中，条目用到的只有 M15 连接注中的 `g0_of_unrestricted`，上文已报。RESULTS 少了 17 个行号，R35 E4 的收据把每个都改名为现存的记录。规范章的定理表、论题、开放计划与词汇表没有对照 C01 读过。10 月 28 日所做的刷新全部成立。

这是作者复读，不是独立验收，不证明新命题。

Board item GC. The last whole-chain reading was A3 (19 October 2026); R35 (20 October) only re-keyed the paths it moved to history and left 120 sources pending. Since then RA's restructure into four layers, R19–R40, DF1, DF1b and DF2's record corrections and the UE and MA landings changed 135 sources this chain fingerprints, and 19 pages joined its discovery globs. Each source was read from the version its fingerprint records, found in git history for all 135 with A2's classifier ([classify_pending.py](../history/root-foundation/development/a2-chain/classify_pending.py), run unchanged at `ba80cb002`), against every entry that links it. [reading.json](development/gc-chain-review/reading.json) lists each source with its kind, entries, recorded commit, current hash, decision and what was found.

**What was read, by kind.** 21 changed only link targets: the move receipts' rewrites take each fingerprint to the current hash (R35's batches and RA's move of the retired chapters, `moves-2026-10-23-moves-ra`), and one is R35's E4 renaming of two record ids. 23 record JSONs changed only bindings, references, aliases, related records, text versions and history (rebinding by R29, R30 and R32, superseded periphery modules re-roled by R35 E8, condensed notes, DF's alias marks); each history line states the claim unchanged, and the claim fields are byte-identical. 33 generated pages: 4 navigation pages and 29 record pages, read through the semantic fields of their records. 14 split by the language separation and changed beyond it, 13 retired chapters and the decision register (round notes moved verbatim to rounds history by R35 E13, R19's restatement of living significance, R36's renumbering, BECOMING-FOUNDATION's §9.1); the further changes of 12 of the chapters were read in full, and those of the canonical chapter and the register only where the entries use them (corrected below). 44 others: 19 records whose fields beyond the bindings changed (DF's corrections, the vocabulary), 17 Lean sources, four retired chapters, RESULTS, GOALS, a verification receipt and the meta-attractor README.

**Corrected 3 November 2026.** The paragraph above said the further changes of all 14 split sources "were read in full", and it did not say that 19 of the 44 others, the 17 Lean sources, RESULTS and GOALS, were read only in part. Both overstated what was read on 28 October. Read in full that day: the changes of 12 of the 14 split chapters (1 to 23 changed lines each). Read only where the entries use them: the retired canonical chapter THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING (R36's restructure, 1,438 lines added and 1,273 removed), at the anchor C03 links (now §6.4, with §6.5) and for the label of C01's link; the decision register (+504 −79), at D43, which M15 and M26 link, and at D45's adoption of where-a-level-couples; the 17 Lean sources (+1,056 −1,054), by the check that every name the chain cites is declared and by the kinds of pass that changed them; GOALS, at the section M20 links; RESULTS, as a page generated from its records. All but the canonical chapter, which was held for A1, were refreshed on that reading. On 3 November the rest was read, each change from the version its fingerprint recorded to `6c84c2d90` ([reread-2026-11-03.json](development/gc-chain-review/reread-2026-11-03.json)):
- *The register, its whole change.* D43's section changed in two link targets only. The one change bearing on an entry is the parenthesis added under D45 to D43's conjecture on observation, answered at the step of the exchange by OB1 (`carried_iff_rising`). That is A3, held on observation-is-a-route.md and applied by GC2 below. [reading.json](development/gc-chain-review/reading.json)'s basis for the register named it A2 by mistake, and now names A3.
- *The 17 Lean sources, line by line.* No theorem an entry names changed its statement. Of the declarations R29, R32 and R37 removed and R30 renamed, the only one any entry uses is `g0_of_unrestricted` in M15's connection note, reported above.
- *GOALS, its whole change.* Besides the rewritten section M20 links: new goals, links moved to history, and the record ids R35 E4 renamed.
- *RESULTS, its whole change.* 17 row ids are gone from the register, and R35 E4's receipt renamed each to a record that exists (alignment-direction-g0-j to alignment-direction-under-widening, wisdom-root-part-one to wisdom-root-without-axiom, and 15 more); no row was lost, and E06 holds.
- *The canonical chapter, where it bears on C01.* The preface, §1.1, §2.5, §9's opening and §11 agree with C01's claim. Its theorem map, theses, open programme and glossary were not read against C01, which links the file as the version it was reviewed against. GC2 recorded this file's fingerprint after reading what changed from `6c84c2d90` on, which is nothing, so that fingerprint rests on these readings.

Every refresh made on 28 October stands.

**Confirmed and recorded, 113.** Every entry that links them holds: C01, C05, C14, C20, C26, C30, F01, F18, F20, F22, F23, F26–F28, F30, E06, M10, M11, M15, M20, M21, M24–M26 as far as these sources go, and the 29 sources no entry links. Three checks over the whole chain: every Lean name the chain cites in backticks is declared, or is a module, in the live Lean tree; of the names the R30 rename table retired, only the label G0 occurs in an entry's rendered text (the relabelling proposal of 19 October), and `g0_of_unrestricted`, now `livingSignificance_of_unrestricted`, occurs once in M15's unrendered connection note, named as the original; every link resolves and validation passes. `generative_chain.py --record-review` recorded the 113 at the versions read.

**Held, 12, for three proposals.** An entry's text would need to change; it is not edited here. Each held source stays pending until the entry is revised or Ren declines the proposal.

- **A1. Living significance and the ethics' two premises** (C03, C17, C24, M15, M20, M21; held: PRACTICAL-JUDGMENT, KNOWLEDGE, ALIGNMENT-DIRECTION, PHILOSOPHICAL-ACCOUNT, ETHICAL-LEARNING, BASIC-CONFLICT-JUDGMENT, WISDOM-AND-CONTEXT and THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING in `theories/becoming/history/`, and open-form-realization-under-revision.json). These entries say that G0, the favourable or positive significance of life and its further generation, "is now a theorem with the condition of section 6.15"; M20 says that actual generative correspondence "feeds G0 positive ground" and M21 that "under G0 … new living can count positively". R19 (`28a97ad91`, 19 October) restated living significance as the Lean shows it, and the nineteenth review's findings E1 and E2 removed the rest: under the continuation condition living significance follows, every living claim having significance from every standpoint (`Valuation.Significant`); it gives significance, not a positive valence, and the `living` readings do not include further generation. The favourable valence and further generation belong to the direction of 生生, read separately by widening what can form (D1). The condition fixes scope and orders nothing; C alone ranks ([07 §3](../../../../theories/becoming/chapters/07-SIGNIFICANCE.md#condition), [07 §4](../../../../theories/becoming/chapters/07-SIGNIFICANCE.md#scope-and-order); 07's "does not show": "That the ethics derives from the condition"). The twentieth review (theme 3), the newcomer reading (finding 3) and AGENTS.md ask every summary of the ethics to name both premises. Section 6.15 is now §6.4 of the retired canonical chapter, and the live home is 07 §3. DF restated open-form-realization-under-revision on the core laws, with the continuation condition, not G0, as its hypothesis. Proposed, in both languages:
  - the status clause in C03, C17, C24, M15, M20 and M21 becomes: "living significance (every living claim has significance from every standpoint) follows under the continuation condition, which the theory does not assert (07 §3); the favourable significance of life and its further generation that G0 stated is that significance read with the direction of 生生 (D1), and ranking it rests on C, calling widening better (07 §4); neither premise is asserted";
  - M20's "actual generative correspondence feeds G0 positive ground" becomes "where each completion corresponds to new living content, the content realized falls, under the continuation condition, under the relevance read from significance" (07 §3.2), and its "positive-ground interface obtaining G0" is restated the same way;
  - M21's "Under G0, consciousness differentiates living significance and new living can count positively" becomes "Under living significance read with the direction of 生生, …", the positive count being that reading's;
  - every "section 6.15" or "§6.15" becomes "07 §3".
  This bears on the [relabelling proposal](../history/root-foundation/CHAIN-RELABELLING-PENDING-REN.md) of 19 October, still unanswered. Its option (a) would rename G0 to living significance in these sentences, and so give living significance the valence and further generation the theory now denies it. A1 should be applied with (a), or before it.
- **A2. The decidability M26's binding laws take** (M26; held: enacted-scene-binding.md, historical-source-binding.md). M26 states, "for every type of records and histories", that a form resolves to a meaning in a repertoire without repeats exactly when every record bearing on the form offers it and every other meaning is missing from some bearing record, and that dated records settle an earlier record's content exactly when every rival is told apart by some dated control. DF (study section 418, 25 October 2026) made both records exact to the Lean: meanings compared decidedly; reports and contents compared decidedly; for the span law, the relation's values compared decidedly with two distinct values. Proposed: "with meanings compared decidedly, a form resolves …" and "with reports and contents compared decidedly, dated records settle …".
- **A3. Observation and dependence, settled** (M26; held: observation-is-a-route.md). M26's scope says "that observation is no second relation beside dependence is a conjecture and is not part of this link". The record now says the question is settled at the step of the exchange: dependence alone carries exactly the responses rising with a level, and a fall only where some level falls ([what-dependence-alone-carries-of-a-level](../../../../theories/becoming/chapters/01-ROOT.md#r-what-dependence-alone-carries-of-a-level), OB1). Read as coupling, by level or by threshold, it holds ([where-a-level-couples](../../../../theories/becoming/chapters/01-ROOT.md#r-where-a-level-couples), adopted in D45). Its statement now reads in coupling terms. Proposed: "Whether observation is a second relation beside dependence is settled at the step of the exchange: dependence alone carries exactly the responses rising with a level (what-dependence-alone-carries-of-a-level), and read as coupling, by level or by threshold, the two are one (where-a-level-couples, D45). Neither is part of this link."

**Left to DF3, 10.** The eight chain-fingerprinted records DF3 is migrating ([plan](../../2026-10-05-repository-reconciliation/architecture/df/DF3-PLAN.md)): derived-history-law-exactness, finite-joining-tree-lifting and growth-constrained-fragmentation (JSON), market-partial-correction-boundary, market-supported-finite-transition and pending-task-context-applicability (pages), recoverable-mediated-change and classification-robust-generative-comparison (both). DF3 reviews them once at its landing; GC recorded none of them.

**Updates proposed, B1–B6.** The entries hold, so their sources are recorded. The text could say more or say it in current words.

- **B1** (C01). NONDUAL-METHOD now separates formation (the forming and transforming of conditions, generative and degenerative alike) from generative (a change that widens what can form), and AGENTS.md forbids reading "generative" as any new organization. C01's "Within generative mechanisms, activity forms and transforms conditions of further activity" uses the older sense; proposed: "Within formation, generative and degenerative alike, activity forms and transforms conditions of further activity".
- **B2** (C01). C01's scope says "the direct inclusion-to-constitution argument remains incomplete". Since XL1–XL4 (22 October) it is argued for the determinations the World premises name: read off one exchange, four ties are internal and 33 central laws hold of what is read off an exchange (BECOMING-FOUNDATION §9.1; [01](../../../../theories/becoming/chapters/01-ROOT.md#r-central-laws-over-every-exchange), `Becoming.OverExchange`). It does not reach every becoming's having such a law, nor the warrant of the readings (locality). The sentence stays true; the scope could say this.
- **B3** (every entry). The chain links none of the eight chapters that now state the theory. 39 links in 29 entries are labelled "current" (C01's "The Theory of Becoming: current chapter and formal spine" among them) and point to chapters RA or R35 retired. Twelve entries link records that have home sections (C01 in 01; C14 in 04 and 05; C30, F01, F22, M15, M21 and M26 mostly in 03; M20, M24, M25 and F30 in 03, 05 and 07). For 17 of the 56 retired files the chain links, RA's [dossiers](../../2026-10-05-repository-reconciliation/architecture/dossiers/) say which chapters took their units (PRACTICAL-JUDGMENT → 07 and 05; KNOWLEDGE → 03 and 07; THE-THEORY-OF-EVOLUTION → 04; ETHICAL-LEARNING → 07 and 05; WISDOM-AND-CONTEXT → 03; and the others). The dossiers list no units for the other 39: 36 were retired before RA, and the live home of what ALIGNMENT-DIRECTION, ALIGNMENT-THROUGH-CHANGE and COGNITION-LEARNING-AND-EVOLUTION carried is chosen by reading. Proposed: give each entry a link to the live home of what it states, keep its history links as the evidence it was reviewed against, and relabel "current" on them as "as reviewed".
- **B4** (C14, C33, F29, M21, M23, M24 and link labels of C14 and M24). DF and the naming study replaced "registration" in the records by coupling, lumping or contacts (finite-fallible-inquiry-necessity, timely-response-necessity, selection-under-registration-revision, consequence-revised-reference-plan, formed-copying-registration). Proposed: the same words in the chain, for example M21's "matching initial registrations" as "alike initial levels of what couples in" and M24's "the registration admits it" as "the link process admits it". DF's [LEFT](../../2026-10-05-repository-reconciliation/architecture/df/LEFT.md) proposes renaming the ids selection-under-registration-revision and formed-copying-registration, which `rename_ids.py` refuses because this chain names them. If Ren approves the renames, this record can take the new ids in C14's and M24's links in the same pass.
- **B5** (new results the chain does not carry). The goal of generative alignment is now the alignment of the joint selection (GOALS, 24 October; [07 §6](../../../../theories/becoming/chapters/07-SIGNIFICANCE.md#alignment-without-premise) on what a selection reaches with no premise; closed-capture, readers-drawn-from-the-selected, shaping-the-rule-selected-by-what-comes-back, loss-read-in-time-by-the-others). The step of invariance is argued (07 §4; widening-as-what-every-weighting-ranks). The selection laws are on the spine ([04](../../../../theories/becoming/chapters/04-LIFE.md#r-evolution-on-descent): Price's equation, Fisher's theorem in its exact-transmission case, the multilevel form, drift and fixation; C14 does not mention them). The formal side of demarcation is stated (what-a-finite-stretch-verifies; M21 says "no universal iff demarcation", still true). M26's transmission laws 8–10 are proved in the periphery with propext, Classical.choice and Quot.sound. Whether these become entries or extend existing scopes is Ren's choice.
- **B6** (the chain's scope). The [significance and prior-art review](BECOMING-SIGNIFICANCE-REVIEW.md) finds no central result to be research-level new mathematics. Each is an unfolding of the definitions or a specialization of a known result. What survives is the premise-by-premise account, the ethics stated with scope and order apart, and one vocabulary across its domains. The chain says nothing of prior art. Proposed: one sentence in its opening scope pointing to [08 §8](../../../../theories/becoming/chapters/08-METHOD.md#known-results).

**The 19 new pages.** Each was read for its role and for any bearing on the entries. None grounds an entry as the chain stands. All join the inspected inventory (`--record-discovery-review`).
- *The eight chapters* (01-ROOT … 08-METHOD, RA, 23 October): the live account, each result stated once in its home section. They ground the results the entries state (B3). Their lists of what they do not show agree with A1: 02 says order needs C, 05 that evaluation is asked only under the two premises, 07 that the ethics does not derive from the condition.
- *Six chapters written 19–22 October and retired by RA* (`theories/becoming/history/`): CHINESE-SOURCES-AND-BECOMING, CIVILIZATION-THOUGHT-AND-BECOMING, THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING-ZHONGHUA, WENLI-AND-BECOMING and YI-ZHUAN-AND-BECOMING, whose content 06 now states, and THE-STEP-OF-INVARIANCE, now 07 §4. They were retired before any entry could link them. The last bears on A1 and B5.
- *Five reviews and studies.* The nineteenth review (in the study's history; its E1 and E2 are the corrections A1 carries into the chain). The twentieth review (theme 3: every summary of the ethics names both premises). The newcomer reading (finding 3: living significance is no free theorem). The significance and prior-art review (B6). The review of the earlier 生生不息 repository (integration candidates, landed separately as SG and X1).

**Rendering.** `--render` does not run while sources are pending: the check fails, by design, on the twelve held sources and DF3's ten. No entry's text changed, and the page generated from the data equals GENERATIVITY-CHAIN.md byte for byte, so the page is current. `--check` lists only the sources held for A1–A3 and those left to DF3.

## DF3 迁移的八项记录：复读与两项提议 / The eight records DF3 migrated: read, and two proposals — 28 October 2026

工作板 DF3（研究第 436 节）。本链所记的八项记录由 DF3 迁移到 schema 2；GC 把它们的 10 个来源留给 DF3。每个来源都对照链接它的条目（C01、M20、M24、M25、F30）复读。8 个来源相合，记入。另有 2 个暂不记入，所附提议 A4、A5 接在 GC 的 A1–A3 之后，按维护规则不改条目：M20 陈述部分纠正的“当且仅当”时未写 0 ≤ β ≤ 3/4 的范围；陈述两端分类时未写其所需的判定。这是作者复读，不是独立验收。

Board item DF3 (study section 436). DF3 migrated eight records this chain fingerprints to schema 2, and GC left their ten sources to DF3 ([section above](#gc-chain-review-28-october-2026)). Each source was read at its landed bytes, against the entries that link it.

**Recorded, 8.** The entries hold as these sources now state them:

- **C01 → derived-history-law-exactness.json.** C01 says transitions are constructed from admitted histories and their summary, and that the actual history language and its warrant remain supplied. The migrated record states the law on the admitted histories of a prefix-closed language. It now also says that every process's feasible words are realized by the law of its histories (`HistoryLanguages.realizes_feasible`), endpoints and branches not kept, which C01 does not deny.
- **M20 → market-supported-finite-transition.md.** M20's "supported finite correction now reaches the original protection region from every positive renewal seed", with "no uniform pulse duration" in its scope, is the record's law: each positive state has its own horizon N.
- **M24 → pending-task-context-applicability.md.** M24 states nothing the migration narrowed. The record now reads the review law for a pending endpoint, as the Lean takes it.
- **M25 and F30 → recoverable-mediated-change.md, and its JSON.** "At nine units, recovery exhausts while activity keeps running and ordinary shutdown is blocked" is the record's exhaustion counterexample, and the stop during recovery is reserved-shutdown-during-recovery's. The JSON is linked by no entry.
- **Linked by no entry:** finite-joining-tree-lifting.json, growth-constrained-fragmentation.json and classification-robust-generative-comparison.json, read for their claims and recorded.

**Held, 2, for two proposals.** M20's text would need to change. It is not edited here, and each source stays pending until M20 is revised or Ren declines the proposal.

- **A4. The range of the partial-correction boundary** (M20; held: market-partial-correction-boundary.md). M20 says "all positive renewal states in the original zero-error model reach protection in finite time iff beta<=5/12". `MarketInstances.lands_iff_margin` and `PartialRegimeTransition.exact_universal_landing_boundary` prove it for 0 ≤ β ≤ 3/4. Proposed: "for 0 ≤ beta ≤ 3/4, all positive renewal states … iff beta ≤ 5/12".
- **A5. The decisions the extreme classifications take** (M20; held: classification-robust-generative-comparison.md). M20 says "strict agreement at the all-secondary and all-basic endpoints covers every mixed assignment". `Lexical.classified_extremes_iff_of_decided` proves it where it is decided whether the alternative below holds each fresh content, and, for each classification, whether it holds every fresh content classified basic. The criterion over every type is exactly excluded middle (the record's sixth law). Proposed: "where the realizations compared are decided, strict agreement at the all-secondary and all-basic endpoints covers every mixed assignment".

Both bear on M20 beside A1, which restates its G0 wording, so they can be applied with A1.

**Left pending, 5.** RESULTS.md (the generated register, which DF3 changes with the thirty statements and other landings since GC changed too) and four sources other landings changed after GC (the pages of organizational-formation-from-generative-comparison, identification-where-runs-part and selection-through-a-model-of-the-options, and Learning.lean) are not DF3's, and are left for the next chain reading.

This is author reading, not independent acceptance, and proves no new claim.

Optionally, with A1, M20's scope can name decided completion: "Decided completion, execution, handoff, generative correspondence and Unrestricted remain explicit" (zh 完成可判定、运行、交接、生成对应与Unrestricted保持显式).
- enacted-scene-binding.md and historical-source-binding.md stay under A2. Both records keep the decisions A2 adds, and now name them in their hypotheses.
